- **v001**: IF invoked directly by human: mode = INTERACTIVE → Follow prompts and manual verification gates normally ``` ([lfernandoss/harness-kit](https://github.com/lfernandoss/harness-kit/tree/HEAD/docs/harness-history/candidates/v001) · 2 copies)
- **v01-三条路线图诊断**: 用户想诊断自己目前在什么财富路线上时；或对"为什么我这么努力还是没钱"感到困惑时；或想从慢车道切换到快车道但不知如何开始时。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E7%99%BE%E4%B8%87%E5%AF%8C%E7%BF%81%E5%BF%AB%E8%BD%A6%E9%81%93/v01-%E4%B8%89%E6%9D%A1%E8%B7%AF%E7%BA%BF%E5%9B%BE%E8%AF%8A%E6%96%AD) · ★1)
- **v01-梦想清单与视觉化**: 当用户没有明确的财务目标时激活。通过列出10个愿望、筛选3个最核心目标、制作梦想相册和视觉化训练，将模糊的愿望转化为行动力。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v01-%E6%A2%A6%E6%83%B3%E6%B8%85%E5%8D%95%E4%B8%8E%E8%A7%86%E8%A7%89%E5%8C%96) · ★1)
- **v01-金钱即人**: 用户在纠结"要不要花这笔钱""该怎么对待钱"时；或想改变自己乱花钱/不尊重小钱的习惯时；或对金钱感到焦虑、想建立正确金钱观时。关键信号："钱就是数字而已"、"小钱无所谓"、"有钱就可以随便花"。不适用于纯财务计算/具体投资操作类问题。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v01-%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E5%8D%B3%E4%BA%BA) · ★1)
- **v016-auto-restart-fix**: V016 Gateway自动重启问题的根因诊断与修复 ([aidong99418/hermes-skills](https://github.com/aidong99418/hermes-skills/tree/HEAD/system/v016-auto-restart-fix))
- **v02-不可控的有限平衡**: 用户想分析一个财富策略/生意模式的数学可行性时；或听到理财建议想判断它是否真的能致富时；或发现自己一直在用时间换金钱想找到根本原因时。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E7%99%BE%E4%B8%87%E5%AF%8C%E7%BF%81%E5%BF%AB%E8%BD%A6%E9%81%93/v02-%E4%B8%8D%E5%8F%AF%E6%8E%A7%E7%9A%84%E6%9C%89%E9%99%90%E5%B9%B3%E8%A1%A1) · ★1)
- **v02-富人四种能力**: 用户想自测自己"会不会理财/有没有能力守住财富"时；或明明赚钱很多却总留不下钱、被坑、挥霍时；或想评估自己的财务短板并补齐时。关键信号："我会赚钱但存不下"、"为什么我这么能赚还是没钱"、"我该补哪方面的财商"。不适用于具体的基金选择/单一操作问题。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v02-%E5%AF%8C%E4%BA%BA%E5%9B%9B%E7%A7%8D%E8%83%BD%E5%8A%9B) · ★1)
- **v02-成功日记与自信训练**: 当用户缺乏自信、需要行动勇气或面临困难决策时激活。通过每日记录成功日记，建立自信和积极的自我认知。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v02-%E6%88%90%E5%8A%9F%E6%97%A5%E8%AE%B0%E4%B8%8E%E8%87%AA%E4%BF%A1%E8%AE%AD%E7%BB%83) · ★1)
- **v03-72小时行动法则**: 当用户决定做某事但一再拖延时激活。强调决定后72小时内完成第一步，否则可能永远不会再做。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v03-72%E5%B0%8F%E6%97%B6%E8%A1%8C%E5%8A%A8%E6%B3%95%E5%88%99) · ★1)
- **v03-可控的无限平衡**: 用户在评估一个创业机会时想分析其数学杠杆时；或想理解"为什么创业者赚得比打工人多那么多"时；或在设计业务模型时想确保变量可控且无限时。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E7%99%BE%E4%B8%87%E5%AF%8C%E7%BF%81%E5%BF%AB%E8%BD%A6%E9%81%93/v03-%E5%8F%AF%E6%8E%A7%E7%9A%84%E6%97%A0%E9%99%90%E5%B9%B3%E8%A1%A1) · ★1)
- **v03-富人标准与独立纪念日**: 用户想知道"我到底算不算有钱人/什么时候才算财务自由"时；或想设定一个清晰的"经济独立"目标并倒推行动计划时；或焦虑自己收入虽高却仍不算富时。关键信号："我年薪多少算有钱？"、"我什么时候才能不工作"、"我算富人了吗"。不适用于具体的投资标的推荐。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v03-%E5%AF%8C%E4%BA%BA%E6%A0%87%E5%87%86%E4%B8%8E%E7%8B%AC%E7%AB%8B%E7%BA%AA%E5%BF%B5%E6%97%A5) · ★1)
- **v04-五条戒律NEEDS检查**: 用户有一个生意/创业想法想快速验证可行性时；或在多个创业方向之间做选择时；或在评估现有业务是否需要调整方向时。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E7%99%BE%E4%B8%87%E5%AF%8C%E7%BF%81%E5%BF%AB%E8%BD%A6%E9%81%93/v04-%E4%BA%94%E6%9D%A1%E6%88%92%E5%BE%8BNEEDS%E6%A3%80%E6%9F%A5) · ★1)
- **v04-复利双刃剑**: 用户在纠结"要不要用贷款/分期/信用卡买理财或消费"时；或想理解为什么"钱生钱"能成、债务却能压垮人时；或想判断一笔储蓄/投资是否划算时。关键信号："复利是不是很厉害"、"为什么房贷越还越没底"、"信用卡分期划算吗"。不适用于宏观行情预测。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v04-%E5%A4%8D%E5%88%A9%E5%8F%8C%E5%88%83%E5%89%91) · ★1)
- **v04-自信与挣钱**: 当用户想挣钱但自我怀疑时激活。自信程度是决定挣钱能力的关键因素，而非聪明程度。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v04-%E8%87%AA%E4%BF%A1%E4%B8%8E%E6%8C%A3%E9%92%B1) · ★1)
- **v05-eval**: V05 outfit recommendation eval — run live scenarios, mine PoolInsufficient logs, multimodal scoring. Surfaces real failures (climate-starved slots, distance-floor exhaustion) and outfit quality regressions. ([auxi-wardrobe/auxi-all-in](https://github.com/auxi-wardrobe/auxi-all-in/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/v05-eval) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **v05-兴趣变现**: 当用户需要增加收入但不知道从何下手时激活。引导用户通过自己喜欢且擅长的事情赚钱。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v05-%E5%85%B4%E8%B6%A3%E5%8F%98%E7%8E%B0) · ★1)
- **v05-固定收入的力量**: 用户收入忽高忽低、想评估自己收入结构质量时；或想理解"为什么稳定现金流比偶然大钱更有价值"时；或收入不稳定的从业者（主播/自由职业/个体户/艺人）想规划收入结构时。关键信号："我收入不稳定怎么办"、"为什么固定工资比项目奖金更有安全感"、"我该把闲钱换成稳定收租的资产吗"。不适用于一次性项目决策。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v05-%E5%9B%BA%E5%AE%9A%E6%94%B6%E5%85%A5%E7%9A%84%E5%8A%9B%E9%87%8F) · ★1)
- **v05-生产者vs消费者思维转换**: 用户发现自己总是站在消费者角度思考问题时；或想培养"像富人一样思考"的能力时；或发现自己的时间都花在消费而非创造上时。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E7%99%BE%E4%B8%87%E5%AF%8C%E7%BF%81%E5%BF%AB%E8%BD%A6%E9%81%93/v05-%E7%94%9F%E4%BA%A7%E8%80%85vs%E6%B6%88%E8%B4%B9%E8%80%85%E6%80%9D%E7%BB%B4%E8%BD%AC%E6%8D%A2) · ★1)
- **v06-金钱引力与种子钱**: 用户想攒钱/存钱但觉得"攒不动、离目标太远"时；或想理解"为什么富人越富"的机制时；或想设定"第一桶金"目标并找到突破口时。关键信号："我什么时候才能攒到第一桶金"、"为什么钱越攒越没劲"、"富人是怎么滚雪球的"。不适用于具体投资产品选择。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v06-%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E5%BC%95%E5%8A%9B%E4%B8%8E%E7%A7%8D%E5%AD%90%E9%92%B1) · ★1)
- **v06-鹅与金蛋**: 当用户想开始储蓄但缺乏动力时激活。通过鹅和金蛋的比喻，让用户理解本金产生被动收入的原理。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v06-%E9%B9%85%E4%B8%8E%E9%87%91%E8%9B%8B) · ★1)
- **v07-收入分配法（50-40-10）**: 当用户发工资后不知道如何分配资金时激活。50%养鹅+40%梦想储蓄罐+10%零花的分配法。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v07-%E6%94%B6%E5%85%A5%E5%88%86%E9%85%8D%E6%B3%95%EF%BC%8850-40-10%EF%BC%89) · ★1)
- **v07-良债vs劣债**: 用户在考虑借钱/贷款（买房、创业、消费贷）时；或想评估自己现有债务是否健康时；或听到"有钱人都负债"而困惑时。关键信号："我该不该贷款买房/创业"、"负债是不是坏事"、"我的房贷算良债还是劣债"。不适用于"怎么还清债务"的具体账务处理（那是预算问题）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v07-%E8%89%AF%E5%80%BAvs%E5%8A%A3%E5%80%BA) · ★1)
- **v08-债务处理四原则**: 当用户面临债务问题时激活。四个核心原则帮助摆脱债务恶性循环。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v08-%E5%80%BA%E5%8A%A1%E5%A4%84%E7%90%86%E5%9B%9B%E5%8E%9F%E5%88%99) · ★1)
- **v08-攒钱机制**: 用户总月光、攒不下钱时；或想建立"攒得住"的机制而不是靠意志力时；或想戒掉信用卡/冲动消费时。关键信号："我工资不低怎么还是月光"、"怎么才能真的存下钱"、"信用卡该不该留"。不适用于收入本身太低的问题（那是 v24 收入问题）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v08-%E6%94%92%E9%92%B1%E6%9C%BA%E5%88%B6) · ★1)
- **v09-72公式（复利速算）**: 当用户需要估算投资收益或通货膨胀影响时激活。72公式快速计算本金翻倍或贬值年数。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E5%B0%8F%E7%8B%97%E9%92%B1%E9%92%B1/v09-72%E5%85%AC%E5%BC%8F%EF%BC%88%E5%A4%8D%E5%88%A9%E9%80%9F%E7%AE%97%EF%BC%89) · ★1)
- **v09-风险与恐惧的逆向**: 用户面对市场暴跌/恐慌想割肉或抄底时；或看到身边人狂热追涨想判断风险时；或想建立"逆向但理性"的投资心态时。关键信号："股市崩了我要不要跑"、"现在是不是抄底时机"、"大家都在买是不是安全"。不适用于宏观行情预测（没人能预测）。 ([R2h1/my-book-skills](https://github.com/R2h1/my-book-skills/tree/HEAD/%E9%87%91%E9%92%B1%E7%9A%84%E5%B1%9E%E6%80%A7%EF%BC%9A%E4%B8%80%E4%B8%AA%E5%AF%8C%E8%B1%AA%E7%9A%84%E8%B4%A2%E5%AF%8C%E7%A7%81%E6%88%BF%E8%AF%BE/v09-%E9%A3%8E%E9%99%A9%E4%B8%8E%E6%81%90%E6%83%A7%E7%9A%84%E9%80%86%E5%90%91) · ★1)

Infere a régua que o próprio app já usa (alinhamento, espaçamento, raio, tipografia) a partir dos valores medidos e aponta onde o app se contradiz: desalinhamentos de 2-3px que o olho sente e não nomeia, espaçamento fora da própria escala, raios misturados, sprawl de tipografia. Mede geometria real no navegador (bounding boxes), separa defeito de decisão, e termina num veredito. Sibling skill to v1b1-capability — reuses its reference.md and vocabulary-lockdown.md by path; never duplicates. - **v1b1-protocol**: Extract a structured command catalog from a vendor device's communication protocol (DLL, server API, or bytestream sniff) so the catalog drops cleanly into a v1b1-author scaffold's command-class slot. Third sibling to v1b1-capability (review) and v1b1-author (scaffold) — the trio covers the full PLR device-authoring lifecycle. Reads reference.md and vocabulary-lockdown.md from v1b1-capability by path; never duplicates. ([vcjdeboer/v1b1-skills](https://github.com/vcjdeboer/v1b1-skills/tree/HEAD/v1b1-protocol))

- **v4-hook-delta-accounting**: Detect Uniswap V4 hooks that fail to settle currency deltas with the PoolManager. Every credit/debit a hook creates (BeforeSwapDelta, afterSwap hookDelta, take/mint, donate, settle/sync) is tracked in the manager's transient nonzeroDeltaCount; if the books aren't flat when unlock returns, the whole transaction reverts (CurrencyNotSettled), and mismatched take/settle/donate either strands hook funds in the manager or lets a swap leave with unpaid debt. Activate on hooks returning deltas, calling take/settle/mint/burn/donate, or custom unlockCallback accounting. ([iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor](https://github.com/iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor/tree/HEAD/rules/uniswap-v4-hooks/skills/v4-hook-delta-accounting) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **v4-hook-generator**: Generate Uniswap v4 hook contracts via OpenZeppelin MCP. ([uniswap/uniswap-ai](https://github.com/uniswap/uniswap-ai) · 719 installs)
- **v4-hook-generator**: Generate Uniswap v4 hook contracts via OpenZeppelin MCP. Use when building custom swap logic, async swaps, hook-owned liquidity, custom curves, dynamic fees, MEV protection, limit orders, or oracle hooks. ([xamie01/SWAT](https://github.com/xamie01/SWAT/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/v4-hook-generator) · 719 installs · 23 copies)
- **v4-hook-permission-flags-mismatch**: Detect Uniswap V4 hooks whose address-encoded permission flags don't match the callbacks the hook actually implements. In V4 the hook's permissions live in the low bits of its deployed address (mined via CREATE2 salt) and must agree with getHookPermissions(); a callback the hook implements but whose flag bit is unset is never invoked, and a flag set without a real implementation makes pool initialization revert in Hooks.validateHookPermissions. Activate on any BaseHook/IHooks contract, getHookPermissions overrides, or hook address mining. ([iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor](https://github.com/iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor/tree/HEAD/rules/uniswap-v4-hooks/skills/v4-hook-permission-flags-mismatch) · ★36 · 2 copies)
- **v4-hook-reentrancy-via-unlock**: Detect reentrancy in Uniswap V4 hooks via the PoolManager unlock/lock callback. V4 uses a singleton PoolManager with transient lock state; all pool mutations happen inside an unlockCallback. A hook that makes external calls during beforeSwap/afterSwap/before*Liquidity (to tokens with hooks, arbitrary routers, or user-controlled contracts) can be re-entered, and because the manager is already unlocked the attacker can recursively swap/modify liquidity against stale hook state. Activate on any V4 hook performing external calls inside a callback, or custom unlockCallback logic. ([iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor](https://github.com/iktok90-design/ai-smart-contract-auditor/tree/HEAD/rules/uniswap-v4-hooks/skills/v4-hook-reentrancy-via-unlock) · ★36 · 2 copies)

- **vaex(超大规模表格分析)**: Use this skill for processing and analyzing large tabular datasets (billions of rows) that exceed available RAM. - **vaex-dataframes**: Out-of-core DataFrame for billion-row data via lazy evaluation and memory-mapped files. Built-in ML transformers (scaling, PCA, K-means). In-memory: polars; distributed: dask. - **vaex-dataframes**: Out-of-core DataFrame library for billion-row datasets using lazy evaluation and memory-mapped files. Supports HDF5, Apache Arrow, Parquet, CSV I/O with cloud storage (S3, GCS, Azure). Built-in ML transformers (scaling, encoding, PCA, K-means) with scikit-learn bridge.
